## `AFG-5621-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Staph-Enterotoxin Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Aviation Food Core Temp Probe Log & Foodborne Pathogen Microbiologist
- **Intro:** Staph-Enterotoxin Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Aviation Food Core Temp Probe Log & Foodborne Pathogen Microbiologist — Pre-Calibration Verification: Ambient temperature difference between probe and reference must be ≤ ±0.1°C. Probe resistance at 0°C must be 100.00 Ω ± 0.05 Ω.
